"""Independent GPT RED-first audit of Macha #528 exact c1d85688: tests ONLY.

Neither source-admission wrapper nor original #528 tests are changed.
RED1: symlinked parent directory escapes supposedly confined data root.
RED2: --run-probe returns ADMITTED after output diverges from the pinned
      committed QC receipt; an output mismatch must not be a success.

These tests use tiny synthetic filesystem fixtures, NEVER a real H5AD, pathology,
outside-allowlist metadata or any learner. No source-rights authority granted.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import unittest

from tests.v5.test_macha_specimen_qc_input_admission_v1 import A, Fixture


class GPT528SourceAdmissionRED(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_r01_parent_directory_symlink_escape_must_refuse(self):
        # The final H5AD file is not a symlink; its parent is. Both files retain
        # original bytes, size, mtime and receipt hashes, so only real-path
        # confinement—not incidental hash drift—can close this case.
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d, tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as outside:
            root = Path(d)
            fixture = Fixture(root)
            outside_data = Path(outside) / "data"
            (root / "data").rename(outside_data)
            (root / "data").symlink_to(outside_data, target_is_directory=True)
            with self.assertRaises(A.AdmissionRefused, msg="admitted data via symlinked parent outside root"):
                fixture.admit()

    def test_r02_probe_output_mismatch_must_fail_closed(self):
        # Existing #528 fixture and the probe itself are SHA-pinned. The only
        # mismatch is the expected output vs the actual probe result; a mismatch
        # must not return a successful admission/replay receipt.
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            root = Path(d)
            fixture = Fixture(root)
            probe = b"import json\nprint(json.dumps({'fixture': 1}))\n"
            fixture.write(A.PROBE, probe)
            pins = dict(fixture.pins, **{
                A.PROBE: hashlib.sha256(probe).hexdigest(),
                A.PROBE_OUTPUT: hashlib.sha256(b'{"fixture": 2}\n').hexdigest(),
            })
            with self.assertRaises(A.AdmissionRefused, msg="probe output differed but run returned an admitted receipt"):
                A.run_probe(root, root / "out.json", pins=pins)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

+def _verified_json_record(payload: Mapping, digest_field: str, label: str) -> dict:
+    """Verify *internal* SHA and detach JSON. NOT external source authentication."""
+    if type(payload) is not dict:
+        raise PostTDContractError(f"{label} must be a plain JSON object")
+    try:
+        snapshot = json.loads(json.dumps(
+            payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True, allow_nan=False
+        ))
+        digest = snapshot.get(digest_field)
+        if type(digest) is not str or not _HEX64.fullmatch(digest):
+            raise PostTDContractError(f"{label} digest is missing or malformed")
+        without_digest = dict(snapshot)
+        del without_digest[digest_field]
+        if _canonical_sha256(without_digest) != digest:
+            raise PostTDContractError(f"{label} digest is not bound to its current content")
+    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError, RecursionError) as exc:
+        if isinstance(exc, PostTDContractError):
+            raise
+        raise PostTDContractError(f"{label} must be canonical JSON") from exc
+    return snapshot
+
+
+def verify_target_portfolio(portfolio: Mapping) -> dict:
+    """Re-derive exact lawful portfolio, not merely its self-rehashable SHA."""
+    value = _verified_json_record(portfolio, "portfolio_sha256", "target portfolio")
+    if value.get("schema") != PORTFOLIO_SCHEMA:
+        raise PostTDContractError("invalid target portfolio schema")
+    qualifications = value.get("qualifications")
+    if type(qualifications) is not dict or any(type(k) is not str for k in qualifications):
+        raise PostTDContractError("portfolio qualifications must be a JSON object with string keys")
+    selected = value.get("selected_candidate")
+    if selected is not None and type(selected) is not str:
+        raise PostTDContractError("selected candidate must be a string or null")
+    foundation = value.get("foundation_candidates")
+    specialists = value.get("specialist_candidates")
+    if type(foundation) is not list or type(specialists) is not list:
+        raise PostTDContractError("portfolio candidate rosters must be lists")
+    if any(type(item) is not str for item in foundation + specialists):
+        raise PostTDContractError("portfolio candidate IDs must be strings")
+    try:
+        rebuilt = build_target_portfolio(
+            qualifications=qualifications,
+            selected_candidate=selected,
+            foundation_candidates=() if selected is not None else foundation,
+            specialist_candidates=specialists,
+        )
+    except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
+        raise PostTDContractError("target portfolio violates its builder's scientific rules") from exc
+    if value != rebuilt:
+        raise PostTDContractError("target portfolio differs from the scientifically rebuilt record")
+    return value
+
+
+def verify_architecture_plan(architecture_plan: Mapping, *, portfolio: Mapping) -> dict:
+    """Rebuild the plan from its declared parameters WITHOUT adding evidence flags."""
+    lawful_portfolio = verify_target_portfolio(portfolio)
+    plan = _verified_json_record(architecture_plan, "architecture_sha256", "architecture plan")
+    if plan.get("schema") != ARCHITECTURE_SCHEMA:
+        raise PostTDContractError("invalid architecture plan schema")
+    ema = plan.get("ema")
+    if type(ema) is not dict:
+        raise PostTDContractError("architecture plan requires an EMA object")
+    half_life = ema.get("half_life_presentations")
+    if type(half_life) is not int:
+        raise PostTDContractError("architecture EMA half-life must be an exact integer")
+    try:
+        rebuilt = build_architecture_plan(
+            portfolio=lawful_portfolio,
+            ema_half_life_presentations=half_life,
+            teacher_topology=plan.get("teacher_topology"),
+            student_topology=plan.get("student_topology"),
+            internal_factorization=plan.get("internal_factorization"),
+            interference_established=False,
+            negative_transfer_established=False,
+            multimodal_authorized=False,
+        )
+    except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
+        raise PostTDContractError("architecture plan violates its builder's scientific rules") from exc
+    if plan != rebuilt:
+        raise PostTDContractError("architecture plan differs from the scientifically rebuilt record")
+    return plan
+

class PostTDHarnessContractTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_independent_c1_c3_blocks_survive_c6_failure(self):
        p = M.build_target_portfolio(
            foundation_candidates=("C1", "C3"),
            qualifications={"C1": "PASS", "C3": "PASS", "C6": "FAIL"},
        )
        self.assertEqual([b["candidate"] for b in p["blocks"]], ["C1", "C3"])
        self.assertEqual([b["block_type"] for b in p["blocks"]], ["SHARED", "QUERY"])
        self.assertIsNone(p["fused_candidate"])

    def test_independent_c1_c2_c3_blocks_survive_c4_failure(self):
        p = M.build_target_portfolio(
            foundation_candidates=("C1", "C2", "C3"),
            qualifications={"C1": "PASS", "C2": "PASS", "C3": "PASS", "C4": "FAIL"},
        )
        self.assertEqual([b["candidate"] for b in p["blocks"]], ["C1", "C2", "C3"])
        self.assertEqual([b["block_type"] for b in p["blocks"]], ["SHARED", "RELATIONAL", "QUERY"])

    def test_c6_cannot_rescue_failed_constituent(self):
        for failed in ("C1", "C3"):
            qualifications = {"C1": "PASS", "C3": "PASS", "C6": "PASS"}
            qualifications[failed] = "FAIL"
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(M.PostTDContractError, failed):
                M.build_target_portfolio(selected_candidate="C6", qualifications=qualifications)

    def test_c4_cannot_rescue_failed_constituent(self):
        for failed in ("C1", "C2", "C3"):
            qualifications = {"C1": "PASS", "C2": "PASS", "C3": "PASS", "C6": "PASS", "C4": "PASS"}
            qualifications[failed] = "FAIL"
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(M.PostTDContractError, failed):
                M.build_target_portfolio(selected_candidate="C4", qualifications=qualifications)

    def test_c5_cannot_be_only_foundation_in_multiblock_api(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(M.PostTDContractError, "specialist"):
            M.build_target_portfolio(
                foundation_candidates=("C5",),
                qualifications={"C5": "PASS"},
            )

    def test_not_estimable_is_never_promoted_to_foundation(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(M.PostTDContractError, "C3"):
            M.build_target_portfolio(
                foundation_candidates=("C1", "C3"),
                qualifications={"C1": "PASS", "C3": "NOT_ESTIMABLE"},
            )

    def test_individual_block_qualification_receipts_are_preserved(self):
        c1 = {"status": "PASS", "receipt_sha256": "1" * 64}
        c3 = {"status": "PASS", "receipt_sha256": "3" * 64}
        p = M.build_target_portfolio(
            foundation_candidates=("C1", "C3"),
            qualifications={"C1": c1, "C3": c3},
        )
        receipts = {b["candidate"]: b["qualification_receipt"] for b in p["blocks"]}
        self.assertEqual(receipts["C1"], c1)
        self.assertEqual(receipts["C3"], c3)

    def test_c6_expands_to_shared_and_query_after_constituent_passes(self):
        p = M.build_target_portfolio(
            selected_candidate="C6",
            qualifications={"C1": "PASS", "C3": "PASS", "C6": "PASS"},
        )

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import unittest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
MODULE_PATH = ROOT / "scripts" / "v5" / "jepa_post_td_harness_contract_v1.py"
SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("jepa_post_td_harness_contract_v1", MODULE_PATH)
assert SPEC and SPEC.loader
M = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
sys.modules[SPEC.name] = M
SPEC.loader.exec_module(M)


class C4RequiresC6Tests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_c4_is_blocked_without_independently_qualified_c6(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(M.PostTDContractError, "C6"):
            M.build_target_portfolio(
                selected_candidate="C4",
                qualifications={
                    "C1": "PASS",
                    "C2": "PASS",
                    "C3": "PASS",
                    "C4": "PASS",
                },
            )

    def test_c4_is_blocked_when_c6_not_estimable_or_failed(self):
        for status in ("FAIL", "NOT_ESTIMABLE"):
            with self.subTest(status=status):
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(M.PostTDContractError, "C6"):
                    M.build_target_portfolio(
                        selected_candidate="C4",
                        qualifications={
                            "C1": "PASS",
                            "C2": "PASS",
                            "C3": "PASS",
                            "C6": status,
                            "C4": "PASS",
                        },
                    )

    def test_c4_remains_available_after_c6_and_all_constituents_pass(self):
        portfolio = M.build_target_portfolio(
            selected_candidate="C4",
            qualifications={
                "C1": "PASS",
                "C2": "PASS",
                "C3": "PASS",
                "C6": "PASS",
                "C4": "PASS",
            },
        )
        self.assertEqual(portfolio["fused_candidate"], "C4")
        self.assertEqual(
            [block["candidate"] for block in portfolio["blocks"]],
            ["C1", "C3", "C2"],
        )

    def test_c4_failure_still_does_not_delete_independent_blocks(self):
        portfolio = M.build_target_portfolio(
            foundation_candidates=("C1", "C2", "C3"),
            qualifications={
                "C1": "PASS",
                "C2": "PASS",
                "C3": "PASS",
                "C6": "PASS",
                "C4": "FAIL",
            },
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            [block["candidate"] for block in portfolio["blocks"]],
            ["C1", "C2", "C3"],
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

class TeacherEvidencePolicyTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_rich_q_blind_teacher_excludes_query_value_only(self):
        visible = C.derive_teacher_evidence_set(
            policy="RICH_Q_BLIND_COMPLEMENTARY",
            measured_addresses={1, 2, 3, 4, 5},
            student_visible_addresses={1, 2},
            query_address=3,
            target_block_addresses={3},
        )
        self.assertEqual(visible, frozenset({1, 2, 4, 5}))

    def test_same_evidence_q_blind_control_gets_no_extra_teacher_evidence(self):
        visible = C.derive_teacher_evidence_set(
            policy="SAME_EVIDENCE_Q_BLIND_CONTROL",
            measured_addresses={1, 2, 3, 4, 5},
            student_visible_addresses={1, 2, 3},
            query_address=3,
            target_block_addresses={3},
        )
        self.assertEqual(visible, frozenset({1, 2}))

    def test_whole_block_blind_excludes_every_block_member(self):
        visible = C.derive_teacher_evidence_set(
            policy="WHOLE_BLOCK_BLIND_CONDITIONAL",
            measured_addresses={1, 2, 3, 4, 5},
            student_visible_addresses={1, 2},
            query_address=None,
            target_block_addresses={3, 4},
        )
        self.assertEqual(visible, frozenset({1, 2, 5}))

    def test_q_visible_reference_keeps_query(self):
        visible = C.derive_teacher_evidence_set(
            policy="RICH_Q_VISIBLE_REFERENCE",
            measured_addresses={1, 2, 3},
            student_visible_addresses={1},
            query_address=3,
            target_block_addresses={3},
        )
        self.assertIn(3, visible)

def build_ema_screen_manifest(
    *,
    architecture_arm: ArchitectureArm,
    half_lives_presentations: Iterable[int],
) -> dict:
    values = [int(x) for x in half_lives_presentations]
    if len(values) != 3 or len(set(values)) != 3 or any(x <= 0 for x in values):
        raise PostTDTournamentError("EMA screen requires exactly three distinct positive half-lives")
    ordered = sorted(values)
    labels = ("SHORT", "INTERMEDIATE", "LONG")
    arms = []
    for label, half_life in zip(labels, ordered):
        C.ema_momentum_for_presentations(1, half_life)
        arms.append({
            "label": label,
            "half_life_presentations": half_life,
            "architecture_arm_id": architecture_arm.arm_id,
        })
    return {
        "schema": "JEPA_POST_TD_EMA_SCREEN_MANIFEST_V1",
        "status": "READY_FOR_AUTHORIZED_EMA_TIMESCALE_SCREEN",
        "native_unit": "SUCCESSFUL_BASE_CELL_PRESENTATIONS",
        "architecture_arm": asdict(architecture_arm),
        "arms": arms,
        "production_half_life_selected": False,
        "historical_fixed_beta_has_authority": False,
        "authority": _authority_off(),
    }


def reduce_architecture_evidence(
    *,
    arm_id: str,
    block_gate_results: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, str]],
    declared_not_estimable: set[str],
    aggregate_score: float | None,
) -> dict:
    if not isinstance(arm_id, str) or not arm_id:
        raise PostTDTournamentError("arm_id required")
    failures: list[str] = []
    declared: list[str] = []
    observed = 0
    for block_id, gate_results in block_gate_results.items():
        if not isinstance(gate_results, Mapping) or not gate_results:
            raise PostTDTournamentError(f"gate results required for block {block_id}")
        for gate, raw_status in gate_results.items():
            key = f"{block_id}:{gate}"
            status = str(raw_status).upper()
            observed += 1
            if status == "PASS":
                continue
            if status == "FAIL":
                failures.append(key)
                continue
            if status == "NOT_ESTIMABLE":
                if key in declared_not_estimable:
                    declared.append(key)
                else:
                    failures.append(key)
                continue
            raise PostTDTournamentError(f"unknown gate result {status!r} for {key}")
    if observed == 0:
        raise PostTDTournamentError("at least one block/gate result is required")
    if failures:
        status = "FAIL"
    elif declared:
        status = "PASS_WITH_DECLARED_NOT_ESTIMABLE"
    else:
        status = "PASS"
    return {
        "schema": "JEPA_POST_TD_ARCHITECTURE_EVIDENCE_REDUCTION_V1",
        "arm_id": arm_id,
        "status": status,
        "failed_block_gates": sorted(failures),
        "declared_not_estimable_block_gates": sorted(declared),
        "aggregate_score": aggregate_score,
        "aggregate_score_has_authority": False,
        "authority": _authority_off(),
    }

_HEX64 = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PresentationEMAState:
    half_life_presentations: int
    successful_presentations: int = 0
    ema_updates: int = 0

    def __post_init__(self):
        if int(self.half_life_presentations) <= 0:
            raise PresentationEMAError("EMA half-life presentations must be positive")
        if int(self.successful_presentations) < 0:
            raise PresentationEMAError("successful presentation count cannot be negative")
        if int(self.ema_updates) < 0:
            raise PresentationEMAError("EMA update count cannot be negative")
        C.ema_momentum_for_presentations(1, int(self.half_life_presentations))


def validate_restart_binding(*, controller, state: PresentationEMAState) -> dict:
    global_step = int(getattr(controller, "global_update_step"))
    ema_count = int(getattr(controller, "ema_update_count"))
    if global_step != ema_count or ema_count != int(state.ema_updates):
        raise PresentationEMAError(
            "EMA restart counter mismatch: "
            f"global_update_step={global_step} controller_ema_updates={ema_count} "
            f"state_ema_updates={state.ema_updates}"
        )
    return {
        "status": "PRESENTATION_EMA_RESTART_BINDING_PASS",
        "global_update_step": global_step,
        "ema_updates": ema_count,
        "successful_presentations": int(state.successful_presentations),
        "half_life_presentations": int(state.half_life_presentations),
    }


def advance_after_proved_optimizer_step(
    *,
    controller,
    state: PresentationEMAState,
    successful_presentations: int,
    proved_optimizer_advanced: bool,
) -> tuple[PresentationEMAState, dict]:
    if proved_optimizer_advanced is not True:
        raise PresentationEMAError("presentation EMA requires a proved optimizer step")
    count = int(successful_presentations)
    if count <= 0:
        raise PresentationEMAError("successful presentations for an EMA update must be positive")
    validate_restart_binding(controller=controller, state=state)
    before_global = int(controller.global_update_step)
    before_ema = int(controller.ema_update_count)
    momentum = C.ema_momentum_for_presentations(count, state.half_life_presentations)
    controller.after_successful_optimizer_step(momentum=momentum)
    after_global = int(controller.global_update_step)
    after_ema = int(controller.ema_update_count)
    if after_global != before_global + 1 or after_ema != before_ema + 1 or after_global != after_ema:
        raise PresentationEMAError(
            "EMA controller did not advance exactly once after the proved optimizer step"
        )
    new_state = PresentationEMAState(
        half_life_presentations=state.half_life_presentations,
        successful_presentations=state.successful_presentations + count,
        ema_updates=state.ema_updates + 1,
    )
    if new_state.ema_updates != after_ema:
        raise PresentationEMAError("presentation EMA state/controller counter mismatch after update")
    return new_state, {
        "schema": "JEPA_POST_TD_PRESENTATION_EMA_UPDATE_V1",
        "native_unit": "SUCCESSFUL_BASE_CELL_PRESENTATIONS",
        "successful_presentations_this_update": count,
        "successful_presentations_total": new_state.successful_presentations,
        "half_life_presentations": new_state.half_life_presentations,
        "momentum": momentum,
        "ema_update_count": new_state.ema_updates,
        "proved_optimizer_advanced": True,
    }


def _validate_sha(value: str, label: str) -> None:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or _HEX64.fullmatch(value) is None:
        raise PresentationEMAError(f"{label} must be lowercase SHA-256")

def _authority_off() -> dict:
    return {
        "target_selection_authorized": False,
        "representation_selection_authorized": False,
        "td60_authorized": False,
        "stage4_authorized": False,
        "training_authorized": False,
        "real_data_authorized": False,
    }


def build_zero_update_teacher_screen(portfolio: Mapping) -> dict:
    # A caller-generated SHA cannot authenticate scientific block semantics.
    portfolio = C.verify_target_portfolio(portfolio)
    blocks = []
    for block in portfolio.get("blocks", ()):
        block_type = str(block.get("block_type", "")).upper()
        blocks.append({
            "block_id": block.get("block_id"),
            "block_type": block_type,
            "policies": list(C.allowed_teacher_policies(block_type)),
            "read_expression_values_before_freeze": False,
            "policy_selection_authorized": False,
        })
    if not blocks:
        raise PostTDTournamentError("target portfolio contains no blocks")
    return {
        "schema": "JEPA_POST_TD_ZERO_UPDATE_TEACHER_SCREEN_V1",
        "status": "READY_FOR_ZERO_UPDATE_TARGET_CONSTRUCTION_SCREEN__NO_TRAINING",
        "portfolio_sha256": portfolio.get("portfolio_sha256"),
        "blocks": blocks,
        "optimizer_updates": 0,
        "ema_updates": 0,
        "authority": _authority_off(),
    }


def supervision_record(state: str) -> dict:
    value = str(state).upper()
    if value == "ELIGIBLE":
        direct = True
    elif value in {"NOT_ESTIMABLE", "TEACHER_PRIVATE"}:
        direct = False
    else:
        raise PostTDTournamentError(f"unknown supervision eligibility state: {value}")
    return {
        "state": value,
        "direct_supervision_allowed": direct,
        "encode_missing_as_zero": False,
    }


_RECOVERABILITY_TO_SEMANTICS = {
    "POINT_IDENTIFIABLE": frozenset({"POINT_STATE", "CONDITIONAL_MEAN"}),
    "STOCHASTIC_IDENTIFIABLE": frozenset({"STATE_PLUS_UNCERTAINTY", "CONDITIONAL_DISTRIBUTION"}),
    "TEACHER_PRIVATE": frozenset({"TEACHER_PRIVATE_CRITIC_ONLY"}),
    "NOT_ESTIMABLE": frozenset({"ABSTAIN_NOT_ESTIMABLE"}),
}


def validate_recoverability_assignment(recoverability: str, semantics: str) -> str:
    r = str(recoverability).upper()
    s = str(semantics).upper()
    if r not in _RECOVERABILITY_TO_SEMANTICS:
        raise PostTDTournamentError(f"unknown recoverability class: {r}")

- Use exact `int` in the three EMA callers.

## New scientific question, not a new dataset or generator
Reuses **exact PR328 frozen world and observation seeds** (20261013/20261014) and V2 world/fit receipt to ask whether a teacher could **actually measure** a fixed q-other-eight panel under source-specific RNA support. This is a **read-only zero-fit fixture diagnostic**, not biological target selection, training or a V3.1.1 production-synthetic experiment.

### Predeclared before first workflow
- Query index 0, **fixed** eight other addresses 1–8, matching only the *shape* of historical R5 measured q-other-8 (not its human gene set).
- A target exists only where q and all 8 others are structurally measured and their panel sum is >0; otherwise `NOT_ESTIMABLE`, never a false measured zero.
- Strict q-excluded `log1p(10k)` other-gene normalization must be unchanged when q count is manipulated.
- Source aggregate-only readout; controller truth, counts, latent, donor IDs and raw target vectors excluded from output.

### Reuse / negative controls
PR311 rare/context/multimodal, PR318 relational package, PR328 generator, PR469 fixture integration, historic V79 Q00–Q11 and rejected PR438 are **not regenerated, fitted, rescored or superseded**. New code is isolated to one measurement diagnostic, hostile tests, and workflow plus non-controlling audit note. The original generator, receipt, latent biology, observation code, controller truth and training code are byte-unchanged.

Run tests plus pinned-runtime source replay. Reject mismatched world/fit receipts, invalid molecular indices, structural zero aliasing, wrong numerical input and q leakage. Every real biology/representation/Stage4/training authority stays OFF. NPH52 synthetic source is NOT real data issuance.

**Review:** independent Macha assessment required for scientific interpretation and data rights; this draft should not be merged as an authoritative biology result.

"""Read-only, NON-AUTHORIZING measurement-support diagnostic on PR328's frozen world.

Not a target generator, encoder, teacher, learner, biological scoring law or
replacement for V79 Q00-Q11 / PR318 / PR438. It reuses the EXACT existing
PR328 default world/fit seeds and receipt, and reports only aggregate support.
The 8-other-gene panel is a source-blind synthetic illustration of R5's
*measured* q-other-8 construction, NOT R5's biological 8-gene panel.

A structural-unmeasured gene is never treated as a measured zero. The queried
scalar is excluded from the measured-panel denominator. No controller truth
or source-human outcome is read. Do not import from any fit/teacher executor.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
import json
from typing import Mapping

import numpy as np

from scripts.v79.unified_synthetic_integration_v1 import UnifiedBiologyConfig, build_unified_biology
from scripts.v79.unified_synthetic_fit_view_v1 import (
    UnifiedObservationConfig, fit_view_sha256, observe_unified_world, serialize_fit_view,
)

SCHEMA = "JEPA_SYNTHETIC_FROZEN_Q_OTHER_8_OBSERVABILITY_V1"
SOURCE_PR328_HEAD = "25bbdbe18aa5ed3c8a39a7e4295bc36de1e328ec"
RECEIPT = "results/v79/unified_synthetic/UNIFIED_SYNTHETIC_INTEGRATION_RECEIPT_V2.json"
WORLD_SEED = 20261013
OBSERVATION_SEED = 20261014
QUERY_INDEX = 0
OTHER_GENE_PANEL = (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8)
SOURCE_NAMES = ("HVS", "NPH52", "SEA_AD")
AUTHORITY_FIELDS = (
    "target_selection_authorized",
    "representation_selection_authorized",
    "td60_authorized",
    "stage4_authorized",
    "training_authorized",
    "real_data_authorized",
    "synthetic_training_authorized",
)
_FORBIDDEN_FIT_KEYS = frozenset({
    "rare_activity", "rare_eligible", "rare_program_activity",
    "rare_donor_prevalence", "expected_molecular_state", "perturbation_state",
    "controller_truth", "controller_truth_sha256", "candidate_mapping",
    "counterfactual_truth", "latent_abundance", "pseudo_rare",
})


def _require(condition: bool, message: str) -> None:
    if not condition:
        raise ValueError("SYNTHETIC_PANEL_MEASUREMENT_REFUSED: " + message)


def measured_panel_target(counts: np.ndarray, support: np.ndarray, *,
                          query_index: int = QUERY_INDEX,
                          panel: tuple[int, ...] = OTHER_GENE_PANEL) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Private diagnostic, not a learner/teacher production interface.

    Returns eligible measurement-row mask and a q-excluded log1p(10k) panel.
    Ineligible targets are NaN and must NOT be scored as zeros.
    """
    _require(type(query_index) is int and query_index >= 0, "exact query index")
    _require(type(panel) is tuple and len(panel) == 8
             and all(type(g) is int and g >= 0 for g in panel)
             and len(set(panel)) == 8 and query_index not in panel, "exact other-gene panel")
    _require(type(counts) is np.ndarray and counts.ndim == 2
             and counts.dtype.kind in "iu", "raw integer count matrix")
    _require(type(support) is np.ndarray and support.dtype == np.dtype("bool")
             and support.shape == counts.shape, "exact Boolean support matrix")
    _require(max((query_index, *panel)) < counts.shape[1], "index outside frozen panel")
    _require(bool(np.all(counts >= 0)), "negative count")
    _require(bool(np.all(counts[~support] == 0)), "unmeasured entries cannot carry count")

    p = np.asarray(panel, dtype=np.int64)
    # Convert to Python integers to avoid NumPy integer-sum overflow.
    denominators = [sum(int(k) for k in row) for row in counts[:, p]]
    measured = support[:, query_index] & support[:, p].all(axis=1)
    positive = np.asarray([d > 0 for d in denominators], dtype=bool)
    eligible = measured & positive
    target = np.full((counts.shape[0], len(panel)), np.nan, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in np.flatnonzero(eligible):
        denom = denominators[int(i)]
        target[i, :] = np.log1p(10000.0 * counts[i, p].astype(np.float64) / denom)
    return eligible, target


def analyze_frozen_fixture(repo_root: Path) -> dict[str, object]:
    root = Path(repo_root).resolve()
    receipt_path = root / RECEIPT
    _require(receipt_path.is_file() and not receipt_path.is_symlink(), "frozen receipt unavailable")
    receipt: Mapping[str, object] = json.loads(receipt_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    _require(receipt.get("schema") == "UNIFIED_SYNTHETIC_INTEGRATION_RECEIPT_V2", "frozen schema")
    authority = receipt.get("authority")
    _require(type(authority) is dict and set(authority) == set(AUTHORITY_FIELDS)
             and all(authority[k] is False for k in AUTHORITY_FIELDS), "frozen authority-off")
    _require(receipt.get("controller_in_fit_package") is False, "controller separation")
    calib = receipt.get("calibration")
    _require(isinstance(calib, dict)
             and calib.get("rare_frequency_source") == "SYNTHETIC_STRESS_PRESETS_NOT_EMPIRICAL_CALIBRATION"
             and calib.get("pseudo_rarity_source") == "SYNTHETIC_STRESS_PRESET_NOT_EMPIRICAL_CALIBRATION"
             and calib.get("stat1_50k_physical_row_status") == "QUARANTINED_NOT_HARD_AUTHORITY",
             "synthetic/nonempirical calibration scope")

    # Reconstruct the existing PR328 receipt world with its ORIGINAL fixed seeds.
    # Never run a fresh scientific tournament or generate a tuned new world.
    world = build_unified_biology(UnifiedBiologyConfig(), seed=WORLD_SEED)
    fit = observe_unified_world(world, UnifiedObservationConfig(), seed=OBSERVATION_SEED)
    _require(world.world_hash == receipt.get("world_sha256"), "frozen world SHA mismatch")
    observed_fit_sha = fit_view_sha256(fit)
    _require(observed_fit_sha == receipt.get("fit_view_sha256"), "frozen fit SHA mismatch")
    payload = serialize_fit_view(fit)
    _require(not (_FORBIDDEN_FIT_KEYS & set(payload)), "controller truth exposed in fit")
    _require(payload["schema"] == "UNIFIED_SYNTHETIC_FIT_VIEW_V1", "fit schema")

    values, support = fit.values, fit.support
    eligible, target = measured_panel_target(values, support)
    # A legal q-other-8 measurement is invariant to the q scalar itself.
    altered = np.asarray(values).copy()
    altered[:, QUERY_INDEX] += 10000
    changed_eligible, changed_target = measured_panel_target(altered, support)
    _require(bool(np.array_equal(eligible, changed_eligible))
             and bool(np.array_equal(target, changed_target, equal_nan=True)),
             "query scalar leaks into other-gene target")

    p = np.asarray(OTHER_GENE_PANEL, dtype=np.int64)
    per_source = {}
    for source in SOURCE_NAMES:
        rows = np.flatnonzero(fit.source == source)
        _require(bool(len(rows)), f"expected fixture source absent: {source}")
        subset = support[rows]
        donor_count = len(np.unique(fit.donor_id[rows]))
        missing = [int(g) for g in p if not bool(subset[:, int(g)].all())]
        support_rows = int((subset[:, QUERY_INDEX] & subset[:, p].all(axis=1)).sum())
        positive_rows = int(eligible[rows].sum())
        per_source[source] = {
            "cells": int(len(rows)),
            "donors": int(donor_count),
            "panel_missing_addresses": missing,
            "fully_measured_8_gene_rows": support_rows,
            "positive_denominator_rows": positive_rows,
            "not_estimable_rows": int(len(rows) - positive_rows),
        }

    return {
        "schema": SCHEMA,
        "generator_head": SOURCE_PR328_HEAD,
        "world_sha256": receipt["world_sha256"],
        "fit_view_sha256": observed_fit_sha,
        "world_seed": WORLD_SEED,
        "observation_seed": OBSERVATION_SEED,
        "query_address_index": QUERY_INDEX,
        "other_gene_panel_address_indices": list(OTHER_GENE_PANEL),
        "construction": "MEASURED_Q_OTHER_8__Q_EXCLUDED_LOG1P10K__STRICT_SUPPORT",
        "q_scalar_invariance": True,
        "n_cells": int(len(fit.source)),
        "per_synthetic_source": per_source,
        "controller_truth_used": False,
        "human_query_rights_authenticated": False,
        "source_labels_are_synthetic": True,
        "fitted_any_model": False,
        "selected_any_target": False,
        "result_scope": "FIXTURE_MEASUREMENT_AVAILABILITY_ONLY__NOT_BIOLOGY_OR_TEACHER_QUALIFICATION",
        "authority": dict(authority),
    }

## Specific new question (distinct from previous fixture/mechanics checks)

**Can a teacher actually construct an R5-type target from eight observed, complementary genes when expression support differs between acquisition sources?** A fitted teacher surrogate cannot answer this measurement prerequisite. Positive pseudo-targets or a hidden controller truth cannot replace lawful measured genes.

**Predeclared test object (not prospective human target selection):** existing PR328 default world `UnifiedBiologyConfig()` seed `20261013`, existing observation `UnifiedObservationConfig()` seed `20261014`, exactly the frozen receipt. For a synthetic query at address index **0**, use the **fixed eight other-gene** panel indices **1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8**. This is a **source-invariant fixed toy panel** illustrating the construction mechanism, not the human R5 eight-gene target and not a biological choice. The existing synthetic visibility code was inspected before this panel was fixed, so this is **not** an independent blinded discovery or a prospective biological validation.

A cell can contribute a measured panel target only when q and all eight other addresses have actual `MEASURED_SCALAR` support and their panel count sum is positive. The target is `log1p(10000 * k[c,j] / sum(k[c,1:8]))` using **measured other genes only**. No q count, source library total or imputed structural zeros enter this normalization. Missing member or zero denominator => **NOT_ESTIMABLE**, never an invented zero or false failure of JEPA. A q-count perturbation must leave the target and eligibility *bit-identical*.

Report ONLY source-level aggregate available/unavailable counts and absent address indices; do not emit per-cell counts, donor IDs, latent biology, controller truth, target vectors, embeddings or fitted results. The source names `HVS`, `NPH52`, `SEA_AD` are **simulation strata**, not real instrument identity or source rights. NPH52 real query issuance remains barred by #513 regardless of synthetic outcome.

## Expected audit use—not result shopping

A concrete failure to construct a panel in one synthetic source is evidence that the **measurement task is not well posed under its frozen visibility geometry**, not that JEPA learned poorly. This tells us to define **source-specific measured target support and teacher-private versus estimable scope** before training, not to change panel/generator after seeing the result. We will not choose an easier replacement panel from test output; any new biological target panel requires separate prospective rights and measurement qualification.

**Success criterion for this diagnostic:** exact frozen world/fit receipt reproduced; valid panel normalization and q-scalar invariance; per-source support and abstentions published as aggregate metadata; original #328 tests inherited; no controller/training/source-rights promotion. **No numerical performance threshold, target PASS, representation selection or estimator scoring**.

def _tile_visibility(n_genes: int) -> np.ndarray:
    base = np.stack([VISIBILITY[s] for s in SOURCES], axis=0)
    reps = int(np.ceil(n_genes / base.shape[1]))
    return np.tile(base, (1, reps))[:, :n_genes]


def _observation_authority(n_genes: int, source_mode: str, family: str, scale: float) -> dict[str, object]:
    # Reuse PR311 source visibility as positional acquisition structure while V79
    # remains the count-realization operator. This affects observation, never biology.
    vis = _tile_visibility(n_genes)
    support = vis > 0
    # Keep a minimal common support so every source has lawful measured addresses.
    support[:, 0] = True
    propensity = np.clip(vis.mean(axis=0) + 0.20, 0.05, None)
    if source_mode == "balanced":
        means = [-0.25, -0.45, -0.65]
        sds = [0.15, 0.18, 0.20]
    else:
        means = [-0.35, -0.85, -0.55]
        sds = [0.18, 0.25, 0.20]
    return {
        "regime_ids": list(SOURCES),
        "structural_support": support.tolist(),
        "capture_log_mean": means,
        "capture_log_sd": sds,
        "gene_propensity": propensity.tolist(),
        "realization_family": family,

**R01 (path confinement).** Every input must be a regular, non-symlink file whose **fully resolved** path stays inside its declared root (data root or evidence root). This closes:
- symlinked or junctioned parents;
- `..` components;
- absolute paths.

All real inputs resolve inside `D:/Jepa project`, so lawful data is unaffected.

**R02 (replay).** `run_probe` writes to `OUT.partial` and publishes only if the LF-normalized output equals the committed #513 output. A probe failure, a mismatch, or an already-existing output each **raise `AdmissionRefused` and leave no file**. A successful replay returns `ADMITTED_AND_REPLAY_MATCHED`.

**Hold enforced in code.** Replaying the real frozen probe raises `REPLAY_HOLD` **before any input is read**, until the QC-only allowlist decision is recorded. The hold is keyed to the real probe pin, so your fixture R02 still exercises the mismatch path and does not pass vacuously.

**Macha independent review of GPT #526 at `0e1c539a8fa5aa2f5e117b6224b535980ed728cb`.** Append-only: no world regenerated with new parameters, no fit, no truth modified.

| Question | Verdict |
|---|---|
| Arithmetic | **REPLICATED** with independent code. World and fit SHAs match receipt V2. HVS-like **0/180**, SEA-AD-like **0/150**, NPH52-like **149/150** (150 fully supported). |
| Software (panel-target construction, q-invariance, NaN abstention) | **ACCEPT_SCOPED** |
| Scientific interpretation | **CORRECTION_REQUIRED** |

**Why the interpretation needs correcting.**
- PR #328's fit view sets `support = VISIBILITY > 0` (`unified_synthetic_fit_view_v1.py:78-81`).
- `VISIBILITY` holds **detection fractions from a 665-cell microglia TRAIN subset** (HVS n=128, NPH52 304, SEA-AD 233). Zero detection among 128 HVS microglia therefore becomes **structural absence** for every HVS-like cell. That breaks `MEASURED_SCALAR != STRUCTURALLY_UNMEASURED`.
- The original rare generator used the same vector correctly, as a detection probability.
- Resulting geometry: HVS-like measures 60.4% of genes, SEA-AD-like 81.3%, NPH52-like 100%. **This is the reverse of the real sources:** HVS and SEA-AD deposit fixed gene matrices, and NPH52 has per-cell-type feature sets.
- The panel was fixed after the visibility code had been read, so the outcome followed from the constants.
- **Do not use the result** to prefer NPH52-like sources, to design source-specific teacher support, or to reason about real estimability.

**Evaluation matrix**
The matrix covers V79/V80, #318→#321, #328, #469 and #526. For each asset it lists:
- mechanism and ground truth;
- what is measurable;
- available controls;
- what was done and adjudicated;
- the missing comparison that could change a decision.

Cross-asset gaps:
- **No wrong-query control exists anywhere.**
- **No estimator or encoder has been scored against #328 or V80 truth.**

**Ranked next steps**
1. An owner/controller ruling on #328 support semantics, decided before any learning test. Any correction goes into a new, prospectively frozen world version; V2 is not edited.
2. The missing independent audit of the #321 phase-2 QUALIFIED receipt `53820343`.
3. Resolve the V79 207-row versus V80 all-291 non-regression conflict in advance.
4. Freeze in advance a right- versus wrong-query and program versus matched-null falsification design, under donor isolation, for the first authorized synthetic JEPA run.

**Preserved:** V79 `NOT_QUALIFIED` (291/292, Q08 hard FAIL); V80 qualifies a benchmark package, not an estimator.

**Evidence:**
- Local replication: Windows, numpy 2.4.6.
- CI (this PR): pinned requirements; it checks the generator is byte-identical to `25bbdbe1` and asserts equality with the local replication and with GPT's published aggregates.

Bash Read the chronology validator rules in full
IN
cd "D:/Jepa project" && MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 git show origin/science/jepa-robust-pipeline-post-jepa-anything-audit-20261013:scripts/v5/verify_jepa_continuous_chronology_20261102.py | sed -n 86,140p

OUT
            "historical Freeze1 declaration missing")
    require(index["milestones"]["historical_stage81a3r_freeze1"].get("current_representation_winner") is False,
            "historical Freeze1 must not promote current dimension")
    for token in ("PR #322", "PR #460", "implementation",
                  "independent review", "frozen", "update"):
        require(token.casefold() in protocol.casefold(), "continuity omission " + token)
    events = data.get("records")
    require(isinstance(events, list) and len(events) >= 3, "missing events")
    ids = []
    prior_date = ""
    required = ("event_id","date_utc","workstream","implementation_pr",
                "implementation_head","source_inputs","change_type",
                "what_already_existed","new_work_or_result",
                "negative_or_counterfactual_evidence","test_review_scope",
                "independent_review","authority_before","authority_after",
                "open_specific_deltas","historical_supersession","evidence_links")
    for event in events:
        require(type(event) is dict and all(k in event for k in required), "event fields")
        require(type(event["event_id"]) is str and event["event_id"].strip(), "event id")
        # A duplicate appended after newer events must be classified as a
        # duplicate, not hidden behind the later date-order check.
        require(event["event_id"] not in ids, "duplicate events")
        ids.append(event["event_id"])
        date = event["date_utc"]
        require(type(date) is str and len(date) >= 10 and date[:10] >= prior_date, "date ordering")
        prior_date = date[:10]
        require(event["change_type"] in TYPES, "unrecognized change type")
        for k in ("workstream","what_already_existed","new_work_or_result",
                  "negative_or_counterfactual_evidence","test_review_scope",
                  "independent_review","historical_supersession"):
            require(type(event[k]) is str and event[k].strip(), "missing " + k)
        require(event["authority_before"] == AUTH and event["authority_after"] == AUTH,
                "authority promotion requires a separate new protocol/version")
        require(type(event["source_inputs"]) is list and event["source_inputs"], "source inputs")
        require(type(event["open_specific_deltas"]) is list, "specific deltas type")
        require(type(event["evidence_links"]) is list and event["evidence_links"], "evidence links")
        require(event["implementation_pr"] is None or
                (type(event["implementation_pr"]) is int and event["implementation_pr"] > 0),
                "implementation PR")
        require(event["implementation_head"] is None or
                (type(event["implementation_head"]) is str and len(event["implementation_head"]) == 40),
                "implementation head")
        for source in event["source_inputs"]:
            require(type(source) is dict and type(source.get("path")) is str and
                    (root / source["path"]).is_file(), "source file identity/availability")
            require(type(source.get("role")) is str and bool(source["role"]), "source role")
    require(len(set(ids)) == len(ids), "duplicate events")
    return {"events": len(events), "historical_sources": len(sources), "authority": AUTH}

if __name__ == "__main__":
    result = check()

The new #526 finding. The synthetic world decides which genes each simulated source "measures" from how often each gene was detected in a small microglia sample (128 HVS cells). Any gene seen in zero of those cells is treated as never measured, so a measured zero becomes structural absence. That reverses the real situation:

In the simulation, NPH52-like measures 100% of genes and HVS-like only 60%.
In reality, HVS and SEA-AD measure a fixed gene list in every cell, and NPH52 is the source with gaps (per-cell-type gene lists).
So #526's result can't guide decisions about real sources.

## 2. Exact first missing integration edge

**A source/operator/query-bound producer of measured observations + physical/private per-cell library provenance into a source-validated successor of Task5 CaseBundle.** This is a qualification and evidence-binding gap, *not* a lack of public dataset access and not a need for a second synthetic generator or target selector.

The existing Task5 `KNOWN_TRUTH_CASEBUNDLE_V1` is explicitly fixture only. Passing a D1 **student** view to it is wrong: D1 contains only visible-base mapped molecule mass; Task5 V1's `library_size` is a physically measured private-source quantity. **Never fill `library_size` by a D1 sum, fake whole-row count, self-supplied unverified total or source-total-derived student depth.** The lawful physical producer and student redaction remain different rights domains.

Task2 has strong donor-access semantics, not automatic source-feature truth. A new source-bound *version* must retain the existing exact Task2 read/producer receipt, bind a source-native feature→41,238-address identity and per-source/per-operator support, alias rights, q-excluded normalization, actual source/permitted cell roster and donor hierarchy, observed count/physical denominator provenance, QC/source selection class, separately generated student D1 and explicitly concealed query scalar. Ineligible cases must yield `NOT_ESTIMABLE`, not a fabricated observed zero or promoted fixture PASS.

**No schema duplication decision:** use #513's defined eight fields and #379's producer→artifact→executor→verdict authority architecture, not a newly invented issuer/gate family. The required CaseBundle source-binding change must be a separately versioned real-path successor to the fixture-only V1, with a **flat main-based code PR** and exact source/parent-resolving tests once the actionable trusted input object and original source mapping are identified. An in-process hash alone cannot certify that real producer; a hardware signer is optional internal independent-actor hardening, not a data download gate. Do not imply source identity is missing (NPH52 Zenodo 8319719 is known).

### Minimal acceptance/refusal tests for that single future implementation

1. **Reject** V2C/D1 view or a D1-as-physical-library substitute presented as real CaseBundle.
2. **Reject** validly shaped but unresolved manifest/policy/access/issuer digest, forged self-declared rights/status/roster or substituted donor/region/matrix keys.
3. **Reject** when query/right-class mapping or structural measurement is unsupported; NPH52 currently abstains for internal alias/selection qualification, not public access.
4. **Reject** query scalar or its indirect library/normalization/selection descendants on the student side; preserve separate physically private teacher/producer support.
5. **Reject** real and Stage4/training authority from synthetic fixtures and self-declared `PASS` even when tests complete.
6. **Accept only after** the producer is independently source/role bound, Task2 raw-reader-split/crossfit manifest and producer-session receipt resolve, the relevant operator's feature/q support and source-cell permissions are qualified, and existing Task5/6/7/8 consumer obligations are satisfied on the same path. This is a future condition, **not currently accomplished**.

No current head inspected presents this complete real producer. The searched source set included main, #513/#528, #379/401, #440/#451/#469 and #465; this is an **exact-source gap assessment, not a claim every one of 824 branches was fully source-reviewed**. Before implementation check subsequently accepted producer PRs and review ancestry to avoid duplicates.

    def test_noncompensation_rejects_failed_required_block(self):

+
+**Class:** AUTHOR ACKNOWLEDGMENT OF INDEPENDENT SCIENTIFIC FALSIFIER. Append-only to PR #526; **no** generator, fit, world, target arithmetic, receipt, frozen threshold or random seed modified. Independent review source: [Macha PR #531](https://github.com/dushyant-mishra/sea-ad-jepa-agent/pull/531) `db0b198dbd00ec13d40052324d18a8d5c3f2fdc3`, original #526 source `0e1c539a8fa5aa2f5e117b6224b535980ed728cb`, original PR328 `25bbdbe18aa5ed3c8a39a7e4295bc36de1e328ec`.
+
+## Binding corrected reading of the old numerical result
+
+Macha independently replicated frozen world hash `dedd53c3b93eecdf28b73f229574a0cda4971371a3a6282f4b9e12fa44d09e66` and fit hash `fc5fa5c071903460da8c0d4644a3b18022f45b178f2c60a727710cadc2fb350a`. The panel's **exact arithmetic** remains: HVS-like 0/180 calculable, SEA-AD-like 0/150, NPH52-like 149/150, one zero-denominator cell. Query-count perturbation leaves the target unchanged and software correctly distinguishes zero denominator vs structural support under the supplied fixture semantics. **That does not establish biological/source-specific measured-target support.**
+
+The original PR328 fit source `scripts/v79/unified_synthetic_fit_view_v1.py`, Git blob `5a5fdd2862dc558683231ed4ef7946efc30c62f1`, lines 69–92, sets `vis = _tile_visibility(n_genes)` and **`support = vis > 0`**. The upstream `VISIBILITY` comes from empirical *detection fractions* among only 665 TRAIN microglia (HVS 128, NPH52 304, SEA-AD 233), not a source's measured feature-list presence. **Zero detected cells is not the same as an unassayed feature.** A gene measurable but zero observed in 128 cells becomes *structurally absent for every source-like cell* by this simulated rule. This violates the scientific distinction `MEASURED_SCALAR with count 0` vs `STRUCTURALLY_UNMEASURED`, and reverses real source geometry: HVS/SEA-AD use deposited fixed gene feature matrices while NPH52 has per-cell-type feature sets.
+
+The observed toy panels were chosen after viewing the visibility code, with indices 1 and 8 absent for HVS-like and 7 for SEA-AD-like; hence the 0/180, 0/150 results follow directly from constants. **Do not prefer NPH52-like, disparage HVS/SEA-AD support, select source-specific target panels, or infer current human C3 estimability from PR526.** It remains a **software-mechanics observation**, and nothing more.
+
+## Scientific decision / successor implementation rule
+
+For every future *source-representative* observed synthetic world:
+1. Define **structural measurement support** from a frozen explicit source/operator feature-universe/cell-type assay-availability table. A feature present in the measured matrix remains measured even if its count equals zero in every sampled cell. Distinguish measured zero, structural absence, collision-unresolved, and withheld rights.
+2. Define **detection probability** (including rarity, acquisition propensity and program-induced positives) separately from structural support; never use `empirical_detection_fraction > 0` as evidence that a feature was assayed or `==0` as proof it was not. The PR311 detection vector may remain an observation-process parameter in that *separate* role only.
+3. Use original #349/#513 source operator and feature evidence to predeclare available experimental support at the next lawful scale. If a source/operator feature table is genuinely missing, emit `NOT_ESTIMABLE` or an explicit *fixture-only unconstrained support* label, not a false biological structural mask.
+4. Any change must be a **new prospectively frozen version**, with its own initial observation design and new world/fit hashes. Do not mutate or requalify frozen PR328 V2 or rerun #526 to rescue its source pattern. Follow controlling #344/#356/#379 and STOP_A/B/C and nonretuning law.
+5. Independent hidden-truth evaluation must include a **wrong-query** negative control (absent across the reviewed synthetic assets), program-vs-matched perturb-null, donor-isolated evaluation, and comparators predeclared before fit. No learned test until the relevant controller and science gates authorize it; this document grants none.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Structural measurement support for the compact-cache genes, at operator (= Stage81A2R matrix) x gene resolution.

MEASURED_SCALAR != STRUCTURALLY_UNMEASURED. The compact analysis cache stores counts only, so an entry whose matrix
does not measure the gene's molecular address looks like a measured zero. This builder joins:

  cache operator index --(S174 shard operator bridge)--> matrix_id
  (matrix_id, molecular_address_index) --(Stage81A2R matrix-level measurement support)--> measured_address

and writes a hash-bound boolean array measured_op_gene[operator row, cache gene]. It fails closed when:
- an input hash differs from its pin;
- an operator is bridged to more than one matrix;
- any (matrix, address) pair is absent from the authority (absence never defaults to measured);
- the measured flag contradicts the measurement status;
- any nonzero count sits on an unmeasured entry (the authority and the cache disagree).

That last custody check reads the stored values of unmeasured entries once, here, only to prove they are zero.
The replay itself never reads them.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import gzip
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

SCHEMA = "V79_G500_STRUCTURAL_SUPPORT_V1"
AUTHORITY_COMMIT = "95d2cafe5cde68773f81c4aa64afc5788ae1d73b"
AUTHORITY_REL = "results/v4/stage81a2r_foundation_molecular_address_measurement_support_candidate.csv.gz"
BRIDGE_REL = "results/v78/V78_S174_SHARD_OPERATOR_BRIDGE_V1.json"
PINS = {
    "cache": "2f922c08e38c104ed7149a2ee74a5e3adc8c287ef5b5c07ea1b5b862f92a6a62",      # G500 compact cache
    "bridge": "b83c35e6940734a7756627d91e3ce982cb4062e9cdeb2fd6035a090abdface92",     # S174 shard operator bridge
    "authority": "ee5d12c144536efdacb983f6b9aa2acb46d47d395b2aa9c556ad10b191f3cdaa",  # Stage81A2R support @95d2cafe
}
UNMEASURED_STATUS = "structurally_unmeasured"


def sha256(path) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as fh:
        for b in iter(lambda: fh.read(1 << 20), b""):
            h.update(b)
    return h.hexdigest()


def array_sha256(a: np.ndarray) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(np.ascontiguousarray(a).tobytes()).hexdigest()


def operator_matrix_map(rows) -> dict[int, str]:
    out: dict[int, set] = {}
    for r in rows:
        out.setdefault(int(r["operator_index"]), set()).add(str(r["matrix_id"]))
    bad = {k: sorted(v) for k, v in out.items() if len(v) != 1}
    if bad:

**Still open (RED in #532; 6 failing sub-cases by design):**
- **M01: the reducer trusts the manifest's own block roster.** A manifest that drops the QUERY block, relabels it SHARED, or flips the rare flag reduces to **PASS** without `QUERY_SPECIFICITY`, `WRONG_Q_CONTROL` or `RARE_PRESERVATION` ever being reported. Nothing ties the manifest to the verified portfolio.

**Recorded limit (L06):** prospective declarations are not time-bound. A manifest rebuilt after the results is accepted. Fixing that needs an external commit point, such as a manifest digest committed before any gate is evaluated, with the reduction naming the digest it used.

**Compatible minimal repair** (throwaway copy, not proposed code):
- the builder embeds the verified portfolio in the manifest and refuses a false rare flag when any block is rare-cross-cutting;
- the reducer re-verifies that portfolio and its roster, and returns `portfolio_sha256`;
- `adjudicate_noncompensation` requires the manifest.

With that, #532 passes 7/7 and **#527 and #530 stay green with no signature change**. Two inherited `adjudicate_noncompensation` expectations need the same migration you applied to the reducer. A new required `portfolio` argument would break the preserved #530 file instead, so embedding is the compatible route.

Bottom line: GPT's #525 repair fixes my three earlier defects but opens one real hole, so it's REPAIR_REQUIRED again. The reducer checks results against whatever manifest it's handed, and nothing ties that manifest to the verified portfolio. So a hand-edited manifest can make the query-leak controls unnecessary. The missing real-data link for the CaseBundle is now pinned down to one small piece of code, but running it on real counts needs your authorization.

1	#525 fresh-head verdict	#532 ccf0239d (tests only, on 0588788f)	41590792957 on Python 3.11 and 3.12: 98/98 existing tests pass; my #527 and #530 tests byte-identical; 6 RED sub-cases fail as designed	REPAIR_REQUIRED, details below

5	Blocker register	#322 33befc30 (event 31)	25/25 ✓; #460 460c3bf5 2/2 ✓	Sorted into completed, implementation, scientific and optional hardening
The #525 problems that remain:

M01: the reducer works out which checks are required from the manifest's own list of blocks. A manifest that drops the query block, relabels it, or turns off the rare-cell flag still produces PASS, even though the query-leak and rare-cell checks were never reported.
M02: a caller can switch off the rare-cell gate, although every verified block requires it.
S01: a second, older pass/fail function has the same gaps (low severity; nothing calls it today).
Recorded limit: a "declared in advance" excuse can still be added after results exist. Code alone can't prove timing; that needs an outside commit point, such as the manifest being pushed before any result.
Fix: I found one that keeps my earlier test files green. Store the verified portfolio inside the manifest, and have the reducer re-check it.
The missing CaseBundle piece. Of the eight fields needed, only two are accepted: the student D1 view (fixture scope) and the Task2 access receipt (interface scope). Raw counts, the per-source support table, cell/donor identity and the private library total all exist but are unaccepted. Alias enforcement and resolvable provenance are specified but not built.

The missing piece is a small adapter that takes one Level-4 block and produces a proper CaseBundle. It keeps the physical library total private and builds the student D1 separately. It's buildable now and tested on synthetic data. Running it on real counts is blocked by three things:

nothing authorizes reading Level-4 count values into a CaseBundle;
the cell selection and QC decisions are still open;
no code yet enforces the alias restrictions you adopted.
I stopped there rather than build a producer that pretends to be authenticated.

Corrections to my own earlier work:

The Phase-2 audit was already done (PR #336). I had listed it as missing.
"The synthetic sources are reversed relative to the real ones" was overstated. Checked against the real support table, out of 41,238 genes, HVS really measures 18,736, SEA-AD 35,786, and NPH52 30,760–34,969 depending on cell type. The real defects are the support mechanism and NPH52's missing gaps; GPT's correction had repeated my wording.
You did decide the alias restrictions (2026-10-30). My earlier erratum called that decision pending; it was made, but nothing in code enforces it.
#523 was accepted by GPT and merged into main on 2026-11-02, so it is no longer an open item.
What I need from you:

The QC-only allowlist decision for the two QC columns.
Whether to authorize reading Level-4 count values into a CaseBundle.
Whether to authorize a corrected synthetic world under the amendment proposal.
